मनुजाःmanujāḥhuman beings (lit. ‘Manu-born’)m. nom. pl. of मनुज; compound (see Samāsa); subject
शताक्षीम्śatākṣīmŚatākṣī, ‘the Hundred-eyed’f. acc. sg. of शताक्षी; compound (see Samāsa); the name, in the accusative with माम्
इतिitias (quotation marker)indeclinable (particle marking a name or quotation)
माम्māmmefirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, acc. sg.; object of कीर्तयिष्यन्ति
ततःtataḥthereforeindeclinable (adverb, correlative of यत्)

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • निरीक्षिष्यामि यन्मुनीन् nirīkṣiṣyāmi yanmunīn → निरीक्षिष्यामि + यत् + मुनीन्
    consonant sandhi (nasalisation) · यरोऽनुनासिकेऽनुनासिको वा (8.4.45)
    Final t (voiced to d at word end, 8.2.39) becomes the nasal n before the m of मुनीन्.
  • शताक्षीमिति śatākṣīmiti → शताक्षीम् + इति
    no change (final m before a vowel) · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23) does not apply before a vowel
    The m is written with the i of इति. Inside the compound, शत + अक्षी → शताक्षी (अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः, 6.1.101).
  • मनुजाः शताक्षीम् manujāḥ śatākṣīm → मनुजाः + शताक्षीम्
    no change (visarga before ś) · खरवसानयोर्विसर्जनीयः (8.3.15)
    Before a sibilant the visarga may stand; the text keeps it here and in ततः शतेन.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • शताक्षीम् śatākṣīm — bahuvrīhi
    Members: शत + अक्षि · Vigraha: शतम् अक्षीणि यस्याः सा शताक्षी, ताम् śatam akṣīṇi yasyāḥ sā śatākṣī, tām
    Literally “she who has a hundred eyes”; in the verse: Śatākṣī, the Hundred-eyed. At the end of a bahuvrīhi अक्षि takes the stem अक्ष (a samāsānta suffix), and the feminine is formed in -ī.
  • मनुजाः manujāḥ — upapada tatpuruṣa
    Members: मनु + ज · Vigraha: मनोः जाताः मनुजाः manoḥ jātāḥ manujāḥ
    Literally “born of Manu”; in the verse: human beings

C. Morphology

  • निरीक्षिष्यामि nirīkṣiṣyāmi — Future of निर् + √ईक्ष् with iṭ (ईक्ष् + इष्य). √ईक्ष् is ātmanepadin, so the classical form would be निरीक्षिष्ये; the parasmaipada ending is an epic and Purāṇic licence (ārṣa).
  • यत् yat — The neuter relative used as a conjunction, ‘since, because’, answered by ततः ‘therefore’ at the end of the verse: यत् … ततः.
  • कीर्तयिष्यन्ति kīrtayiṣyanti — Future of the curādi stem कीर्तय- (कीर्तय + इष्य + अन्ति); with माम् … शताक्षीम् इति, ‘they will proclaim me as Śatākṣī’.

D. Anvaya — prose order

ततः यत् (अहं) नेत्राणां शतेन मुनीन् निरीक्षिष्यामि, ततः मनुजाः मां शताक्षीम् इति कीर्तयिष्यन्ति।

tataḥ yat (ahaṃ) netrāṇāṃ śatena munīn nirīkṣiṣyāmi, tataḥ manujāḥ māṃ śatākṣīm iti kīrtayiṣyanti |

A causal clause (यत् … निरीक्षिष्यामि) and its correlative main clause (ततः … कीर्तयिष्यन्ति); इति marks the name.

6Translation

LiteralThen, since I shall look upon the sages with a hundred eyes, men will therefore proclaim me as ‘Śatākṣī’.

ReadableBecause I shall look upon the sages with a hundred eyes, men will call me Śatākṣī, the Hundred-eyed.

The first ततः (‘then’) is left untranslated in tr; ‘the Hundred-eyed’ glosses the name.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningThe first name of the third descent: she looks upon the sages with a hundred eyes, and so is called Śatākṣī. The text gives only the gaze; it does not say what the eyes do.

Traditional interpretationLater Śākta retellings fill the gap. In the Devī Bhāgavata Purāṇa the Hundred-eyed Goddess, seeing the suffering of the drought, weeps from all her eyes, and her tears become the rain that refills the rivers. The Devī Māhātmya itself says only that she looked.

Modern reflectionA modern reading: the first thing the Goddess does in the famine is to see. Attention comes before relief.

Textual Verification Required / uncertain: यत्: read as the conjunction ‘since’, answered by ततः. It could also be taken as ‘when’ (‘when I look upon the sages …’); the sense is nearly the same.

11.48Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

ततोऽहमखिलं लोकमात्मदेहसमुद्भवैः।
भरिष्यामि सुराः शाकैरावृष्टेः प्राणधारकैः ॥ ४८ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

ततः | अहम् | अखिलम् | लोकम् | आत्मदेहसमुद्भवैःभरिष्यामि | सुराः | शाकैः | आ | वृष्टेः | प्राणधारकैः

3IAST

As recitedtato'hamakhilaṃ lokamātmadehasamudbhavaiḥ |
bhariṣyāmi surāḥ śākairāvṛṣṭeḥ prāṇadhārakaiḥ ||

Separatedtataḥ | aham | akhilam | lokam | ātmadehasamudbhavaiḥ
bhariṣyāmi | surāḥ | śākaiḥ | ā | vṛṣṭeḥ | prāṇadhārakaiḥ

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
ततःtataḥthenindeclinable (adverb of time)
अहम्ahamIfirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, nom. sg.; subject
अखिलम्akhilamentire, wholem. acc. sg. of अखिल; qualifies लोकम्
लोकम्lokamworldm. acc. sg. of लोक; object of भरिष्यामि
आत्मदेहसमुद्भवैःātmadehasamudbhavaiḥarisen from my own bodym. instr. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies शाकैः
भरिष्यामिbhariṣyāmiI shall sustain, I shall nourish√भृ (juhotyādi, 3U ‘to bear, support’), lṛṭ (future), uttama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
सुराःsurāḥO godsm. voc. pl. of सुर; address
शाकैःśākaiḥwith vegetables, with edible plantsm. instr. pl. of शाक; instrument of भरिष्यामि
आāuntilindeclinable (preposition, karmapravacanīya of limit, governing the ablative)
वृष्टेःvṛṣṭeḥthe rainf. abl. sg. of वृष्टि (i-stem; √वृष् + ktin); governed by आ
प्राणधारकैःprāṇadhārakaiḥlife-sustainingm. instr. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies शाकैः

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • ततोऽहमखिलं tato'hamakhilaṃ → ततः + अहम् + अखिलम्
    visarga sandhi; vowel sandhi (pūrvarūpa) · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66), अतो रोरप्लुतादप्लुते (6.1.113), आद्गुणः (6.1.87), एङः पदान्तादति (6.1.109)
    -aḥ before a becomes -o, and the following a is absorbed (avagraha). The m of अहम् is written with अखिलम्.
  • लोकमात्मदेहसमुद्भवैः lokamātmadehasamudbhavaiḥ → लोकम् + आत्मदेहसमुद्भवैः
    no change (final m before a vowel) · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23) does not apply before a vowel
    The m is written with the ā of आत्म-.
  • शाकैरावृष्टेः śākairāvṛṣṭeḥ → शाकैः + आ + वृष्टेः
    visarga sandhi (r before a vowel) · ससजुषो रुः (8.2.66)
    After a vowel other than a/ā, the visarga (as ru) appears as r before a vowel: शाकै + र् + आ. आ and वृष्टेः are simply written together.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • आत्मदेहसमुद्भवैः ātmadehasamudbhavaiḥ — bahuvrīhi containing a ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa (आत्मदेह)
    Members: आत्मदेह + समुद्भव · Vigraha: आत्मनः देहः आत्मदेहः; आत्मदेहात् समुद्भवः येषां ते, तैः ātmanaḥ dehaḥ ātmadehaḥ; ātmadehāt samudbhavaḥ yeṣāṃ te, taiḥ
    Literally “whose origin is from one’s own body”; in the verse: grown from my own body
    Alternative: Also read as a pañcamī tatpuruṣa, आत्मदेहात् समुद्भवाः ‘arisen from my body’; the sense is the same.
  • प्राणधारकैः prāṇadhārakaiḥ — ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa (object genitive with an agent noun in -aka)
    Members: प्राण + धारक · Vigraha: प्राणानां धारकाः, तैः prāṇānāṃ dhārakāḥ, taiḥ
    Literally “holders of the life-breaths”; in the verse: life-sustaining

C. Morphology

  • भरिष्यामि bhariṣyāmi — Future of √भृ: roots in ṛ take the connecting vowel i in the future (भर् + इष्य + आमि). ‘I shall bear, sustain’ — the verb from which the name Śākambharī (11.49) is made.
  • आ ā — आ (आङ्) with the ablative marks a limit: आ वृष्टेः ‘until the rain’. The text writes it joined (आवृष्टेः); it is not a compound, since वृष्टेः keeps its own case ending.

D. Anvaya — prose order

(हे) सुराः, ततः अहम् आ वृष्टेः आत्मदेहसमुद्भवैः प्राणधारकैः शाकैः अखिलं लोकं भरिष्यामि।

(he) surāḥ, tataḥ aham ā vṛṣṭeḥ ātmadehasamudbhavaiḥ prāṇadhārakaiḥ śākaiḥ akhilaṃ lokaṃ bhariṣyāmi |

Subject अहम्, verb भरिष्यामि, object अखिलं लोकम्; the instrument शाकैः with two epithets; आ वृष्टेः gives the limit of time.

6Translation

LiteralThen I, O gods, shall sustain the whole world, until the rain, with life-sustaining vegetables arisen from my own body.

ReadableThen, O gods, until the rains return, I shall feed the whole world with life-giving plants grown from my own body.

‘Until the rains return’ renders आ वृष्टेः, ‘until the rain’; ‘feed’ renders भरिष्यामि, ‘sustain, nourish’.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningIn the drought the Goddess feeds the world from her own body: edible plants that keep life going until the rain comes. The verse prepares her name in 11.49, Śākambharī, ‘she who bears (the world with) vegetables’.

Traditional interpretationThe image of the Goddess as the one who feeds is old in Śākta devotion: Śākambharī is worshipped as the giver of food in famine. The verse itself is precise about the limit — आ वृष्टेः, only until the rain — the Goddess sustains, and the ordinary order resumes.

Textual Verification Required / uncertain: The printed आवृष्टेः is divided here as आ + वृष्टेः (‘until the rain’), the reading of the usual translations. Taking आवृष्टि as one noun (‘rainfall’, ablative ‘from the rain’) makes poorer sense of the verse.

11.49Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

शाकम्भरीति विख्यातिं तदा यास्याम्यहं भुवि।
तत्रैव च वधिष्यामि दुर्गमाख्यं महासुरम् ॥ ४९ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

शाकम्भरी | इति | विख्यातिम् | तदा | यास्यामि | अहम् | भुवितत्र | एव | च | वधिष्यामि | दुर्गमाख्यम् | महासुरम्

3IAST

As recitedśākambharīti vikhyātiṃ tadā yāsyāmyahaṃ bhuvi |
tatraiva ca vadhiṣyāmi durgamākhyaṃ mahāsuram ||

Separatedśākambharī | iti | vikhyātim | tadā | yāsyāmi | aham | bhuvi
tatra | eva | ca | vadhiṣyāmi | durgamākhyam | mahāsuram

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
शाकम्भरीśākambharīŚākambharī, ‘she who sustains with vegetables’f. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); the name, cited in the nominative before इति
इतिitias (quotation marker)indeclinable (particle marking a name)
विख्यातिम्vikhyātimfame, renownf. acc. sg. of विख्याति (वि + √ख्या + ktin); goal of यास्यामि
तदाtadāthenindeclinable (adverb of time)
यास्यामिyāsyāmiI shall go (to), I shall attain√या (adādi, 2P ‘to go’), lṛṭ (future), uttama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
अहम्ahamIfirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, nom. sg.; subject
भुविbhuvion earthf. loc. sg. of भू
तत्रtatrathereindeclinable (adverb of place, तद् + tral)
एवevaright, justindeclinable (emphatic particle); with तत्र, ‘in that very place, at that same time’
चcaandindeclinable (conjunction)
वधिष्यामिvadhiṣyāmiI shall slay√वध् (the stem वध, used for √हन्), lṛṭ (future), uttama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
दुर्गमाख्यम्durgamākhyamnamed Durgamam. acc. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies महासुरम्
महासुरम्mahāsuramgreat asuram. acc. sg.; compound (karmadhāraya, as in 11.44); object of वधिष्यामि

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • शाकम्भरीति śākambharīti → शाकम्भरी + इति
    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    ī + i → ī.
  • यास्याम्यहं yāsyāmyahaṃ → यास्यामि + अहम्
    vowel sandhi (yaṇ) · इको यणचि (6.1.77)
    i before the unlike vowel a becomes y. The m of अहम् becomes anusvāra before bh (मोऽनुस्वारः, 8.3.23).
  • तत्रैव tatraiva → तत्र + एव
    vowel sandhi (vṛddhi) · वृद्धिरेचि (6.1.88)
    a + e → ai.
  • दुर्गमाख्यं durgamākhyaṃ → दुर्गम + आख्यम्
    vowel sandhi inside a compound (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    a + ā → ā; the final m is anusvāra before the m of महासुरम् (8.3.23).

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • शाकम्भरी śākambharī — upapada tatpuruṣa (√भृ with the suffix khac, which inserts m after the first member; feminine in -ī)
    Members: शाक + (म्) + भरी · Vigraha: शाकान् बिभर्ति इति शाकम्भरी śākān bibharti iti śākambharī
    Literally “she who bears vegetables”; in the verse: she who sustains the world with plants (11.48)
  • दुर्गमाख्यम् durgamākhyam — bahuvrīhi
    Members: दुर्गम + आख्या · Vigraha: दुर्गमः आख्या यस्य सः, तम् durgamaḥ ākhyā yasya saḥ, tam
    Literally “whose name is Durgama”; in the verse: the asura called Durgama, ‘hard to approach’

C. Morphology

  • वधिष्यामि vadhiṣyāmi — Pāṇini’s grammar uses वध as a replacement of √हन् only in the aorist and the benedictive; a future वधिष्यामि is not classical but is common in epic and Purāṇic Sanskrit (ārṣa). It recurs in 11.53.
  • यास्यामि yāsyāmi — Future of √या (aniṭ: या + स्य + आमि). With विख्यातिम्, ‘I shall go to fame’, i.e. become famous.

D. Anvaya — prose order

तदा अहं भुवि शाकम्भरी इति विख्यातिं यास्यामि। तत्र एव च दुर्गमाख्यं महासुरं वधिष्यामि।

tadā ahaṃ bhuvi śākambharī iti vikhyātiṃ yāsyāmi | tatra eva ca durgamākhyaṃ mahāsuraṃ vadhiṣyāmi |

Two clauses: (1) अहं … विख्यातिं यास्यामि with the name in an इति phrase; (2) तत्र एव च … वधिष्यामि with its object.

6Translation

LiteralThen I shall go to renown on earth as ‘Śākambharī’; and right there I shall slay the great asura named Durgama.

ReadableThen I shall be famed on earth as Śākambharī, and in that same descent I shall slay the great asura called Durgama.

‘In that same descent’ interprets तत्र एव, ‘right there’.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningThe second name of the third descent: Śākambharī, ‘she who sustains with plants’, from the deed of 11.48. In the same appearance (तत्र एव) she slays a great asura named Durgama, whose name gives her third name in 11.50.

Traditional interpretationThe text says nothing more of Durgama. Later Śākta retellings, such as the Devī Bhāgavata Purāṇa, make him the cause of the drought: having taken the Vedas from the gods by a boon, he stopped the sacrifices, and the rains failed. The Devī Māhātmya gives only the name and the slaying.

11.50Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

दुर्गा देवीति विख्यातं तन्मे नाम भविष्यति।
पुनश्चाहं यदा भीमं रूपं कृत्वा हिमाचले ॥ ५० ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

दुर्गा | देवी | इति | विख्यातम् | तत् | मे | नाम | भविष्यतिपुनः | च | अहम् | यदा | भीमम् | रूपम् | कृत्वा | हिमाचले

3IAST

As reciteddurgā devīti vikhyātaṃ tanme nāma bhaviṣyati |
punaścāhaṃ yadā bhīmaṃ rūpaṃ kṛtvā himācale ||

punaḥ | ca | aham | yadā | bhīmam | rūpam | kṛtvā | himācale

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • दुर्गा देवीति durgā devīti → दुर्गा + देवी + इति
    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    ī + i → ī (देवी + इति → देवीति).
  • विख्यातं तन्मे vikhyātaṃ tanme → विख्यातम् + तत् + मे
    anusvāra; consonant sandhi (nasalisation) · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23); यरोऽनुनासिकेऽनुनासिको वा (8.4.45)
    Final m becomes anusvāra before t; final t (voiced to d at word end, 8.2.39) becomes n before the nasal m of मे.
  • पुनश्चाहं punaścāhaṃ → पुनः + च + अहम्
    visarga sandhi; vowel sandhi · विसर्जनीयस्य सः (8.3.34), स्तोः श्चुना श्चुः (8.4.40); अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    Visarga before c becomes s, then ś; च + अहम् → चाहम् (a + a → ā). The m of अहम् is anusvāra before y (8.3.23).
  • हिमाचले himācale → हिम + अचले
    vowel sandhi inside a compound (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    a + a → ā.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • हिमाचले himācale — ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa
    Members: हिम + अचल · Vigraha: हिमस्य अचलः हिमाचलः, तस्मिन् himasya acalaḥ himācalaḥ, tasmin
    Literally “on the mountain of snow”; in the verse: on the Himālaya
    Alternative: Also analysed as a karmadhāraya with the middle member dropped, हिमयुक्तः अचलः ‘the snowy mountain’; the referent is the same.

C. Morphology

  • दुर्गा durgā — The name is given from the deed of 11.49: she who slew Durgama is called Durgā. As a word, दुर्ग (दुस् + √गम्, ‘hard to reach’) with the feminine -ā; the text connects it with the asura’s name, not with this etymology.
  • विख्यातम् vikhyātam — Past participle of वि + √ख्या, neuter to agree with नाम: ‘(that name) will become famous’.
  • कृत्वा kṛtvā — Absolutive of √कृ with -त्वा: ‘having made (a form)’, i.e. assuming it. The clause it begins is completed in 11.51.

D. Anvaya — prose order

‘दुर्गा देवी’ इति तत् मे नाम विख्यातं भविष्यति। पुनः च यदा अहं हिमाचले भीमं रूपं कृत्वा (11.51: रक्षांसि भक्षयिष्यामि …)

‘durgā devī’ iti tat me nāma vikhyātaṃ bhaviṣyati | punaḥ ca yadā ahaṃ himācale bhīmaṃ rūpaṃ kṛtvā (11.51: rakṣāṃsi bhakṣayiṣyāmi …)

The first line closes the third descent: तत् मे नाम (subject) विख्यातं भविष्यति, with the name in an इति phrase. The second line opens a new यदा-clause (the fourth descent) whose verb is in 11.51.

6Translation

Literal‘Durgā Devī’ — thus that name of mine will become renowned. And again, when I, having assumed a terrible form on the Himālaya, —

ReadableThat name of mine, Durgā Devī, will be renowned. And again, when I take a terrible form on the Himālaya,

The second line is the beginning of a sentence completed in 11.51–52.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningThe third name of the same descent: having slain Durgama (11.49), the Goddess will be known as Durgā Devī. The second line opens the fourth descent: a terrible form on the Himālaya, completed in the next verse.

Traditional interpretationHere the text explains the name Durgā by a deed — the slaying of Durgama — not by the meaning ‘hard to approach’. The name has been used of her throughout the Devī Māhātmya; the prophecy gives it its story.

Comparative Śākta / ŚrīvidyāLalitā Sahasranāma (Brahmāṇḍa Purāṇa) has दुर्गमा and दुर्गा as consecutive names, 189 and 190. The pairing is shared Śākta vocabulary; the Sahasranāma does not tell the story of Durgama.

11.51Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

रक्षांसि भक्षयिष्यामि मुनीनां त्राणकारणात्।
तदा मां मुनयः सर्वे स्तोष्यन्त्यानम्रमूर्तयः ॥ ५१ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

रक्षांसि | भक्षयिष्यामि | मुनीनाम् | त्राणकारणात्तदा | माम् | मुनयः | सर्वे | स्तोष्यन्ति | आनम्रमूर्तयः

3IAST

As recitedrakṣāṃsi bhakṣayiṣyāmi munīnāṃ trāṇakāraṇāt |
tadā māṃ munayaḥ sarve stoṣyantyānamramūrtayaḥ ||

Separatedrakṣāṃsi | bhakṣayiṣyāmi | munīnām | trāṇakāraṇāt
tadā | mām | munayaḥ | sarve | stoṣyanti | ānamramūrtayaḥ

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
रक्षांसिrakṣāṃsithe rākṣasas, demonsn. acc. pl. of रक्षस् (s-stem); object of भक्षयिष्यामि
भक्षयिष्यामिbhakṣayiṣyāmiI shall devour√भक्ष् (curādi, 10, stem भक्षय- ‘to eat’), lṛṭ (future), uttama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada; verb of the यदा-clause begun in 11.50
मुनीनाम्munīnāmof the sagesm. gen. pl. of मुनि; with त्राणकारणात्
त्राणकारणात्trāṇakāraṇātfor the sake of protectionn. abl. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); ablative of cause
तदाtadāthenindeclinable (correlative of यदा in 11.50)
माम्māmmefirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, acc. sg.; object of स्तोष्यन्ति
मुनयःmunayaḥthe sagesm. nom. pl. of मुनि; subject
सर्वेsarveallpronominal adjective सर्व, m. nom. pl.; qualifies मुनयः
स्तोष्यन्तिstoṣyantiwill praise√स्तु (adādi, 2U ‘to praise’), lṛṭ (future), prathama-puruṣa bahuvacana, parasmaipada
आनम्रमूर्तयःānamramūrtayaḥwith bodies bowed lowm. nom. pl.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies मुनयः

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • मुनीनां त्राणकारणात् munīnāṃ trāṇakāraṇāt → मुनीनाम् + त्राणकारणात्
    anusvāra · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23)
    Final m becomes anusvāra before the consonant t. The same in मां मुनयः.
  • स्तोष्यन्त्यानम्रमूर्तयः stoṣyantyānamramūrtayaḥ → स्तोष्यन्ति + आनम्रमूर्तयः
    vowel sandhi (yaṇ) · इको यणचि (6.1.77)
    i before the unlike vowel ā becomes y.
  • मुनयः सर्वे munayaḥ sarve → मुनयः + सर्वे
    no change (visarga before s) · खरवसानयोर्विसर्जनीयः (8.3.15)
    Before a sibilant the visarga may stand; the text keeps it.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • त्राणकारणात् trāṇakāraṇāt — ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa
    Members: त्राण + कारण · Vigraha: त्राणस्य कारणम्, तस्मात् trāṇasya kāraṇam, tasmāt
    Literally “from the cause of protection”; in the verse: for the sake of protecting (the sages)
    Alternative: Also read as a karmadhāraya, त्राणम् एव कारणम् ‘protection being the reason’; the sense is the same.
  • आनम्रमूर्तयः ānamramūrtayaḥ — bahuvrīhi
    Members: आनम्र + मूर्ति · Vigraha: आनम्राः मूर्तयः येषां ते ānamrāḥ mūrtayaḥ yeṣāṃ te
    Literally “whose bodies are bowed down”; in the verse: bowing low in reverence

C. Morphology

  • स्तोष्यन्ति stoṣyanti — Future of √स्तु (aniṭ): guṇa स्तो + स्य, s → ṣ after o (स्तोष्य-), with -अन्ति. It recurs in 11.54.
  • रक्षांसि rakṣāṃsi — Neuter plural of रक्षस्: the s-stem lengthens its vowel and inserts a nasal (anusvāra) before -सि in the nom./acc. plural.

D. Anvaya — prose order

(यदा अहं हिमाचले भीमं रूपं कृत्वा) मुनीनां त्राणकारणात् रक्षांसि भक्षयिष्यामि, तदा सर्वे मुनयः आनम्रमूर्तयः (सन्तः) मां स्तोष्यन्ति,

(yadā ahaṃ himācale bhīmaṃ rūpaṃ kṛtvā) munīnāṃ trāṇakāraṇāt rakṣāṃsi bhakṣayiṣyāmi, tadā sarve munayaḥ ānamramūrtayaḥ (santaḥ) māṃ stoṣyanti,

The first line completes the यदा-clause of 11.50 (verb भक्षयिष्यामि); the second is its correlative तदा-clause, whose sentence ends with the name in 11.52.

6Translation

Literal— shall devour the rākṣasas for the protection of the sages, then all the sages, with bodies bowed low, will praise me;

Readableand devour the rākṣasas to protect the sages, then all the sages will bow low and praise me,

The participial compound आनम्रमूर्तयः is rendered as a verb (‘bow low’). The sentence runs from 11.50 to 11.52.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningThe fourth descent: on the Himālaya, in a terrible form, the Goddess devours rākṣasas to protect the sages, and the sages bow and praise her. The name follows in 11.52.

Traditional interpretationAs in the third descent, the occasion is the sages’ need; each form in this prophecy is called down by a particular distress and named by those it saves.

11.52Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

भीमा देवीति विख्यातं तन्मे नाम भविष्यति।
यदारुणाख्यस्त्रैलोक्ये महाबाधां करिष्यति ॥ ५२ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

भीमा | देवी | इति | विख्यातम् | तत् | मे | नाम | भविष्यतियदा | अरुणाख्यः | त्रैलोक्ये | महाबाधाम् | करिष्यति

3IAST

As recitedbhīmā devīti vikhyātaṃ tanme nāma bhaviṣyati |
yadāruṇākhyastrailokye mahābādhāṃ kariṣyati ||

Separatedbhīmā | devī | iti | vikhyātam | tat | me | nāma | bhaviṣyati
yadā | aruṇākhyaḥ | trailokye | mahābādhām | kariṣyati

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
भीमाbhīmāBhīmā, ‘the Terrible’f. nom. sg. of भीमा; the name, cited in the nominative before इति
देवीdevīthe Goddessf. nom. sg.; part of the cited name ‘Bhīmā Devī’
इतिitithus, as (quotation marker)indeclinable (particle marking a name)
विख्यातम्vikhyātamrenownedpast participle (kta) of वि + √ख्या, n. nom. sg.; predicate of नाम (as in 11.50)
तत्tatthatpronoun तद्, n. nom. sg.; qualifies नाम
मेmemyfirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, enclitic gen. sg.
नामnāmanamen. nom. sg. of नामन्; subject of भविष्यति
भविष्यतिbhaviṣyatiwill be√भू (bhvādi, 1P), lṛṭ (future), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
यदाyadāwhenindeclinable (relative adverb of time); answered by तदा in 11.53
अरुणाख्यःaruṇākhyaḥthe one named Aruṇam. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); subject of करिष्यति
त्रैलोक्येtrailokyein the three worldsn. loc. sg. of त्रैलोक्य (taddhita, as in 11.39)
महाबाधाम्mahābādhāmgreat affliction, great oppressionf. acc. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); object of करिष्यति
करिष्यतिkariṣyatiwill cause, will do√कृ (tanādi, 8U), lṛṭ (future), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • भीमा देवीति bhīmā devīti → भीमा + देवी + इति
    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    ī + i → ī, as in 11.50.
  • विख्यातं तन्मे vikhyātaṃ tanme → विख्यातम् + तत् + मे
    anusvāra; consonant sandhi (nasalisation) · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23); यरोऽनुनासिकेऽनुनासिको वा (8.4.45)
    As in 11.50.
  • यदारुणाख्यस्त्रैलोक्ये yadāruṇākhyastrailokye → यदा + अरुणाख्यः + त्रैलोक्ये
    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha); visarga sandhi · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101); विसर्जनीयस्य सः (8.3.34)
    ā + a → ā (यदा + अरुण- → यदारुण-); visarga before t becomes s.
  • महाबाधां करिष्यति mahābādhāṃ kariṣyati → महाबाधाम् + करिष्यति
    anusvāra · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23)
    Final m becomes anusvāra before k.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • अरुणाख्यः aruṇākhyaḥ — bahuvrīhi
    Members: अरुण + आख्या · Vigraha: अरुणः आख्या यस्य सः aruṇaḥ ākhyā yasya saḥ
    Literally “whose name is Aruṇa”; in the verse: an asura called Aruṇa
  • महाबाधाम् mahābādhām — karmadhāraya
    Members: महत् + बाधा · Vigraha: महती चासौ बाधा च, ताम् mahatī cāsau bādhā ca, tām
    Literally “a great affliction”; in the verse: grievous oppression of the worlds

C. Morphology

  • करिष्यति kariṣyati — Future of √कृ (roots in ṛ take iṭ: कर् + इष्य + ति): ‘(he) will do, will cause’. With बाधाम्, ‘will cause affliction’.

D. Anvaya — prose order

(तदा) ‘भीमा देवी’ इति तत् मे नाम विख्यातं भविष्यति। यदा अरुणाख्यः त्रैलोक्ये महाबाधां करिष्यति, (11.53: तदा अहं …)

(tadā) ‘bhīmā devī’ iti tat me nāma vikhyātaṃ bhaviṣyati | yadā aruṇākhyaḥ trailokye mahābādhāṃ kariṣyati, (11.53: tadā ahaṃ …)

The first line closes the sentence of 11.50–51 with the name; the second opens the fifth descent with a यदा-clause whose correlative is in 11.53.

6Translation

Literal‘Bhīmā Devī’ — thus that name of mine will become renowned. When the one named Aruṇa causes great affliction in the three worlds, —

Readableand my name will be renowned as Bhīmā Devī. When the asura called Aruṇa brings great oppression on the three worlds,

‘Asura’ is supplied; the text gives only the name Aruṇa, but 11.53 calls him महासुरम्.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningThe fourth form is named: Bhīmā Devī, ‘the Terrible Goddess’, from the terrible form of 11.50. The second line opens the fifth and last named descent: an asura called Aruṇa will afflict the three worlds.

Traditional interpretationThe line ‘… इति विख्यातं तन्मे नाम भविष्यति’ is repeated word for word from 11.50, a formula that seals each naming. The repeated formula is how the prophecy keeps its shape as a list of names.

11.53Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

तदाहं भ्रामरं रूपं कृत्वाऽसंख्येयषट्पदम्।
त्रैलोक्यस्य हितार्थाय वधिष्यामि महासुरम् ॥ ५३ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

तदा | अहम् | भ्रामरम् | रूपम् | कृत्वा | असंख्येयषट्पदम्त्रैलोक्यस्य | हितार्थाय | वधिष्यामि | महासुरम्

3IAST

As recitedtadāhaṃ bhrāmaraṃ rūpaṃ kṛtvā'saṃkhyeyaṣaṭpadam |
trailokyasya hitārthāya vadhiṣyāmi mahāsuram ||

Separatedtadā | aham | bhrāmaram | rūpam | kṛtvā | asaṃkhyeyaṣaṭpadam
trailokyasya | hitārthāya | vadhiṣyāmi | mahāsuram

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
तदाtadāthenindeclinable (correlative of यदा in 11.52)
अहम्ahamIfirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, nom. sg.; subject
भ्रामरम्bhrāmaramof bees, bee-liken. acc. sg. of भ्रामर (taddhita aṇ from भ्रमर ‘bee’); qualifies रूपम्
रूपम्rūpamformn. acc. sg. of रूप; object of कृत्वा
कृत्वाkṛtvāhaving assumed (lit. ‘having made’)absolutive (ktvā) of √कृ (tanādi, 8U)
असंख्येयषट्पदम्asaṃkhyeyaṣaṭpadammade of countless beesn. acc. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies रूपम्
त्रैलोक्यस्यtrailokyasyaof the three worldsn. gen. sg. of त्रैलोक्य; with हितार्थाय
हितार्थायhitārthāyafor the welfarem. dat. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); dative of purpose
वधिष्यामिvadhiṣyāmiI shall slay√वध् (the stem वध, used for √हन्), lṛṭ (future), uttama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada (as in 11.49)
महासुरम्mahāsuramthe great asuram. acc. sg.; compound (karmadhāraya, as in 11.44); object of वधिष्यामि (Aruṇa)

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • तदाहं tadāhaṃ → तदा + अहम्
    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    ā + a → ā; the m of अहम् becomes anusvāra before bh (8.3.23).
  • कृत्वाऽसंख्येयषट्पदम् kṛtvā'saṃkhyeyaṣaṭpadam → कृत्वा + असंख्येयषट्पदम्
    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    ā + a → ā. The Gita Press writes an avagraha after the ā to show where the initial a of असंख्येय- was absorbed; it is a reading aid, not a separate sound.
  • हितार्थाय hitārthāya → हित + अर्थाय
    vowel sandhi inside a compound (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    a + a → ā.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • असंख्येयषट्पदम् asaṃkhyeyaṣaṭpadam — bahuvrīhi (its members: असंख्येय, a nañ tatpuruṣa न संख्येयः; षट्पद, a bahuvrīhi षट् पादाः यस्य सः, ‘six-footed’ = bee)
    Members: असंख्येय + षट्पद · Vigraha: असंख्येयाः षट्पदाः यस्मिन् तत् asaṃkhyeyāḥ ṣaṭpadāḥ yasmin tat
    Literally “(a form) in which the six-footed ones are countless”; in the verse: a form made of innumerable bees
  • हितार्थाय hitārthāya — caturthī tatpuruṣa (with अर्थ, always compounded)
    Members: हित + अर्थ · Vigraha: हिताय अयम् हितार्थः, तस्मै hitāya ayam hitārthaḥ, tasmai
    Literally “for the purpose of welfare”; in the verse: for the good (of the three worlds)

C. Morphology

  • भ्रामरम् bhrāmaram — Taddhita with vṛddhi of the first syllable: भ्रमर → भ्रामर ‘belonging to bees, consisting of bees’. The feminine भ्रामरी becomes her name in 11.54.
  • वधिष्यामि vadhiṣyāmi — Purāṇic future of the stem वध, as in 11.49.

D. Anvaya — prose order

तदा अहं असंख्येयषट्पदं भ्रामरं रूपं कृत्वा त्रैलोक्यस्य हितार्थाय (तं) महासुरं वधिष्यामि।

tadā ahaṃ asaṃkhyeyaṣaṭpadaṃ bhrāmaraṃ rūpaṃ kṛtvā trailokyasya hitārthāya (taṃ) mahāsuraṃ vadhiṣyāmi |

The correlative तदा-clause of 11.52: the absolutive phrase (… रूपं कृत्वा), the purpose (त्रैलोक्यस्य हितार्थाय), the verb वधिष्यामि and its object.

6Translation

Literal— then I, having assumed a bee-form of countless six-footed ones, shall slay the great asura for the welfare of the three worlds.

Readablethen I shall take a form of countless bees and slay that great asura for the good of the three worlds.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningThe fifth descent: against Aruṇa the Goddess takes a form made of innumerable bees and kills him for the welfare of the three worlds. The form is a swarm, not a single body.

Traditional interpretationLater Śākta retellings, such as the Devī Bhāgavata Purāṇa, explain the form: Aruṇa had a boon that no two- or four-footed creature could kill him, so the Goddess came as six-footed bees. The Devī Māhātmya itself gives no reason for the form.

11.54Ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

भ्रामरीति च मां लोकास्तदा स्तोष्यन्ति सर्वतः।
इत्थं यदा यदा बाधा दानवोत्था भविष्यति ॥ ५४ ॥

2Padaccheda — the words separated

भ्रामरी | इति | च | माम् | लोकाः | तदा | स्तोष्यन्ति | सर्वतःइत्थम् | यदा | यदा | बाधा | दानवोत्था | भविष्यति

3IAST

As recitedbhrāmarīti ca māṃ lokāstadā stoṣyanti sarvataḥ |
itthaṃ yadā yadā bādhā dānavotthā bhaviṣyati ||

Separatedbhrāmarī | iti | ca | mām | lokāḥ | tadā | stoṣyanti | sarvataḥ
ittham | yadā | yadā | bādhā | dānavotthā | bhaviṣyati

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
भ्रामरीbhrāmarīBhrāmarī, ‘the Goddess of the bees’f. nom. sg. of भ्रामरी (feminine of the taddhita भ्रामर, 11.53); the name, cited in the nominative before इति
इतिitias (quotation marker)indeclinable (particle marking a name)
चcaandindeclinable (conjunction)
माम्māmmefirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, acc. sg.; object of स्तोष्यन्ति
लोकाःlokāḥpeople, the worldsm. nom. pl. of लोक; subject
तदाtadāthenindeclinable (adverb of time)
स्तोष्यन्तिstoṣyantiwill praise√स्तु (adādi, 2U), lṛṭ (future), prathama-puruṣa bahuvacana, parasmaipada (as in 11.51)
सर्वतःsarvataḥeverywhere, on all sidesindeclinable (सर्व + tas)
इत्थम्itthamthus, in this wayindeclinable (adverb of manner)
यदाyadāwhenindeclinable (relative adverb of time)
यदाyadā(when)everindeclinable; repeated for distribution: यदा यदा ‘whenever’, answered by तदा तदा in 11.55
बाधाbādhāaffliction, oppressionf. nom. sg. of बाधा; subject of भविष्यति
दानवोत्थाdānavotthāarising from the Dānavasf. nom. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); qualifies बाधा
भविष्यतिbhaviṣyatiwill arise, will be√भू (bhvādi, 1P), lṛṭ (future), prathama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • भ्रामरीति bhrāmarīti → भ्रामरी + इति
    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    ī + i → ī.
  • लोकास्तदा lokāstadā → लोकाः + तदा
    visarga sandhi · विसर्जनीयस्य सः (8.3.34)
    Visarga before t becomes s.
  • इत्थं यदा itthaṃ yadā → इत्थम् + यदा
    anusvāra · मोऽनुस्वारः (8.3.23)
    Final m becomes anusvāra before the consonant y.
  • दानवोत्था dānavotthā → दानव + उत्था
    vowel sandhi inside a compound (guṇa) · आद्गुणः (6.1.87)
    a + u → o.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • दानवोत्था dānavotthā — upapada tatpuruṣa (pañcamī: the source in the ablative; उत्थ from उद् + √स्था with the suffix -a, feminine -ā)
    Members: दानव + उत्था · Vigraha: दानवेभ्यः उत्तिष्ठति इति दानवोत्था dānavebhyaḥ uttiṣṭhati iti dānavotthā
    Literally “rising from the Dānavas”; in the verse: trouble caused by the demons

C. Morphology

  • भ्रामरी bhrāmarī — Feminine in -ī of भ्रामर (11.53): ‘she of the bees’. The name is taken from the form, as Raktadantikā and Śatākṣī were.
  • दानवोत्था dānavotthā — उत्था: उद् + √स्था, the s of स्था lost after उद् (उत्था-), with the agent suffix -a: ‘that which rises up’.

D. Anvaya — prose order

तदा च लोकाः सर्वतः मां ‘भ्रामरी’ इति स्तोष्यन्ति। इत्थं यदा यदा दानवोत्था बाधा भविष्यति, (11.55: तदा तदा …)

tadā ca lokāḥ sarvataḥ māṃ ‘bhrāmarī’ iti stoṣyanti | itthaṃ yadā yadā dānavotthā bādhā bhaviṣyati, (11.55: tadā tadā …)

The first line closes the fifth descent with the name; the second begins the closing generalisation, a distributive यदा यदा-clause whose correlative तदा तदा is in 11.55.

6Translation

LiteralAnd then the people everywhere will praise me as ‘Bhrāmarī’. Thus, whenever affliction arising from the Dānavas shall occur, —

Readableand people everywhere will then praise me as Bhrāmarī. Thus, whenever oppression born of the demons arises,

‘Demons’ renders दानव (the sons of Danu); the sentence is completed in 11.55.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningThe fifth form is named: Bhrāmarī, ‘she of the bees’, praised everywhere. With इत्थम्, ‘thus’, the Goddess turns from the list of named descents to a rule: whenever demons cause affliction.

Traditional interpretationAfter the daughter of Yaśodā (11.42) the prophecy has named six forms in four descents: Raktadantikā; Śatākṣī, Śākambharī and Durgā (one descent, 11.46–50); Bhīmā; and Bhrāmarī. The Mūrtirahasya recited after the Saptaśatī gives meditations on these forms.

11.55Half-ślokaanuṣṭubh (śloka)

1Original śloka

तदा तदावतीर्याहं करिष्याम्यरिसंक्षयम् ॥ ५५ ॥ ॐ

2Padaccheda — the words separated

तदा | तदा | अवतीर्य | अहम् | करिष्यामि | अरिसंक्षयम्

3IAST

As recitedtadā tadāvatīryāhaṃ kariṣyāmyarisaṃkṣayam ||

Separatedtadā | tadā | avatīrya | aham | kariṣyāmi | arisaṃkṣayam

4Word by word

SanskritIASTMeaningGrammar
तदाtadāthenindeclinable (correlative of यदा in 11.54)
तदाtadā(then) each timeindeclinable; repeated for distribution, answering यदा यदा: ‘each time then’
अवतीर्यavatīryahaving descendedabsolutive (lyap) of अव + √तॄ (bhvādi, 1P), as in 11.43
अहम्ahamIfirst-person pronoun अस्मद्, nom. sg.; subject
करिष्यामिkariṣyāmiI shall bring about√कृ (tanādi, 8U), lṛṭ (future), uttama-puruṣa ekavacana, parasmaipada
अरिसंक्षयम्arisaṃkṣayamthe destruction of the enemiesm. acc. sg.; compound (see Samāsa); object of करिष्यामि

5Vyākaraṇa

A. Sandhi and sandhi-viccheda

  • तदावतीर्याहं tadāvatīryāhaṃ → तदा + अवतीर्य + अहम्
    vowel sandhi (savarṇa-dīrgha) · अकः सवर्णे दीर्घः (6.1.101)
    ā + a → ā twice: तदा + अवतीर्य → तदावतीर्य, अवतीर्य + अहम् → अवतीर्याहम्. The m becomes anusvāra before k (8.3.23).
  • करिष्याम्यरिसंक्षयम् kariṣyāmyarisaṃkṣayam → करिष्यामि + अरिसंक्षयम्
    vowel sandhi (yaṇ) · इको यणचि (6.1.77)
    i before the unlike vowel a becomes y.

B. Samāsa and samāsa-vigraha

  • अरिसंक्षयम् arisaṃkṣayam — ṣaṣṭhī tatpuruṣa
    Members: अरि + संक्षय · Vigraha: अरीणां संक्षयः, तम् arīṇāṃ saṃkṣayaḥ, tam
    Literally “the complete destruction of enemies”; in the verse: the ending of the foes (of the worlds and the gods)

C. Morphology

  • तदा tadā — Repetition for distribution (vīpsā): यदा यदा … तदा तदा ‘whenever … each time then’. The doubled word makes a single promise into a standing rule.
  • करिष्यामि kariṣyāmi — Future of √कृ with iṭ (कर् + इष्य + आमि). With अरिसंक्षयम्, ‘I shall make an end of the enemies’ — the last verb of the chapter.

D. Anvaya — prose order

(यदा यदा दानवोत्था बाधा भविष्यति,) तदा तदा अहम् अवतीर्य अरिसंक्षयं करिष्यामि॥

(yadā yadā dānavotthā bādhā bhaviṣyati,) tadā tadā aham avatīrya arisaṃkṣayaṃ kariṣyāmi ||

The correlative clause of 11.54b: the doubled तदा तदा, the absolutive अवतीर्य, the verb करिष्यामि and its object.

6Translation

Literal— then, each time, having descended, I shall bring about the destruction of the enemies.

Readableeach time I shall descend and destroy the enemies.

The half-śloka completes the sentence begun in 11.54; ‘each time’ renders the doubled तदा.

7Commentary

Direct textual meaningThe prophecy closes in a rule: whenever demons afflict the worlds, the Goddess will descend (अवतीर्य) and destroy the enemies. This answers the gods’ request of 11.39 exactly: एवम् एव, ‘in just this way’, every time. A half-śloka, counted by the Gita Press as its own mantra; the closing ॐ belongs to the recitation.

Traditional interpretationThe construction recalls the Bhagavadgītā’s promise of descent (4.7–8, यदा यदा हि धर्मस्य …): the same यदा यदा and the same idea of a recurring descent. Here the one who descends is the Goddess, and the occasion is the oppression of the worlds by demons.

Modern reflectionA modern reading: the chapter ends not with a final victory but with a promise of return. The text treats protection as a pattern that repeats, not a single event.

Colophon

इति श्रीमार्कण्डेयपुराणे सावर्णिके मन्वन्तरे देवीमाहात्म्ये देव्याः स्तुतिर्नामैकादशोऽध्यायः॥

iti śrīmārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe sāvarṇike manvantare devīmāhātmye devyāḥ stutirnāmaikādaśo'dhyāyaḥ||
